---------------------------------------------------------------------
Turn 1: ocram6 deploys Foxparks' Harness, activates it; Flambelle's attack on Trxvor's Pengullet nullified by Cryolinx interrupt (1 soul). Turn 2: ocram6 deploys Pengullet (4 souls) and Single-Shot Sphere Launcher (3 souls), draws 1. Turn 3: Trxvor's Pengullet (500) destroys Flambelle (400); Pengullet attacks for 2 (9->7 life). Turn 4: ocram6 deploys Suzaku; Trxvor's Pengullet takes 900 dmg destroyed; Suzaku (1200) destroys Pengullet (500). Turn 5: ocram6 deploys Chillet (5 souls), reveals Pengullet; deploys Pengullet Rocket Launcher (4 souls), buffs a Pengullet +500/explode +200, deals 1300 to own Pals then sacrifices it. Turn 6: Trxvor's Bushi interrupts Chillet's attack. Turn 7: ocram6 deploys Primitive Furnace, Flambelle, activates Furnace twice, deploys Single-Shot Rifle (Trxvor's Chillet takes 1500 dmg, destroyed), deploys Foxparks, activates rifle (+200 power), Foxparks attack cancelled by Lucky Pal. Turn 8: ocram6 deploys Pengullet (3 souls, +500/explode), deals 1000 to own Pals then sacrifices; deploys Blazehowl Noct (6 souls). Turn 9: Trxvor's Blazehowl Noct attacks for 2 (7->5 life). Turn 10: ocram6 activates Furnace, deploys Single-Shot Rifle (Blazehowl Noct takes 1500, destroyed), deploys Bushi; Bushi's attack on Trxvor nullified by Reindrix interrupt (1 soul). Turn 11: ocram6 deploys Lyleen Noct (8 souls, discards Reindrix as cost), deploys Chillet from graveyard (rested), reveals Pengullet Rocket Launcher; deploys Foxparks, activates Foxparks' Harness and Single-Shot Rifle twice (+200 each); Lyleen Noct takes 700 dmg; Foxparks (1000) destroys Trxvor's Chillet (900); ocram6 plays Axel's Strategy, Lyleen Noct takes 1500 dmg destroyed. Turn 12: ocram6 deploys Relaxaurus (7 souls), plays Zoe's Strategy (3 souls) returning Chillet, discards Foxparks, plays Pal Sphere, deploys Foxparks, activates Harness+Rifle twice (+200 each), Relaxaurus takes 700 dmg; Foxparks hits Trxvor for 2 (9->7 life). Turn 13: ocram6 deploys Chillet (5 souls), reveals Strike from the Darkness, plays it (4 souls). Turn 14: Trxvor's Blazehowl interrupts Chillet's attack. Turn 15: ocram6 deploys Suzaku (Trxvor's Chillet takes 900, destroyed), activates Rifle twice (+200 each), Suzaku hits Trxvor for 3 (7->4 life). Turn 16: ocram6 deploys Astegon (8 souls); all Pals with Power<=300 destroyed. Turn 17: Trxvor's Blazehowl interrupts Astegon's attack; Bushi interrupts Relaxaurus's attack. Turn 18: ocram6 deploys Flambelle, activates Furnace, deploys Ragnahawk, activates Rifle twice (+200 each); Ragnahawk (1900) destroys Trxvor's Relaxaurus (1200). Turn 19: ocram6 deploys Pengullet (4 souls, +500/explode), draws 1, deals 1100 to own Pals then sacrifices; plays Zoe's Strategy (3 souls) returning Chillet, discards Reptyro; all Pals<=300 power destroyed. Turn 20: Trxvor's Astegon attack cancelled by Lucky Pal. Turn 21: ocram6 deploys Jormuntide Ignis, attacks Trxvor for 4, cancelled by Lucky Pal. Turn 22: ocram6 plays Strike from the Darkness (4 souls), deploys Chillet (5 souls), reveals Zoe's Strategy. Turn 23: Trxvor's Chillet attacks for 2, ocram6's life 5->3; all Pals<=300 power destroyed; Astegon attacks for 3, life hits 3->0.

Turn 4: xNIGHTn deploys Mounted Machine Gun (4 souls) and Ragnahawk (6 souls), assigns Rooby (buff), buffs all LavenousCAM's Red Pals +500/+1 strike (odd effect, likely a card ability triggered). Turn 5: Ragnahawk's attack interrupted by Reindrix. Turn 6: xNIGHTn plays Aurora Guide, deploys Lyleen Noct, activates it (discards Victor's Strategy as cost), deploys Relaxaurus; Ragnahawk rested; Relaxaurus (1200) destroys LavenousCAM's Mounted Machine Gun (1000). Turn 7: xNIGHTn deploys Foxparks (2 souls), Rooby (2 souls, assign trigger+buff+draw), Stone Pit (1 soul, another Rooby trigger+buff+draw), Mounted Machine Gun (4 souls, assigns Foxparks); plays Strike from the Darkness (Ragnahawk destroyed as cost), deploys Elphidran; its attack on LavenousCAM's Foxparks nullified by Foxparks interrupt (1 soul). Turn 8: xNIGHTn buffs Foxparks repeatedly via Rooby assigns and draws. Turn 9: LavenousCAM's Foxparks attacks xNIGHTn for 1 (10->9 life); deploys Jormuntide Ignis (8 souls); its attack interrupted by Blazehowl Noct; Rooby assign buffs+draws. Turn 10: xNIGHTn plays Victor's Strategy, tutors 1 of 5. Turn 11: LavenousCAM's Jormuntide Ignis interrupted by Cryolinx, rests 3 souls to pay cost then stands it, attacks xNIGHTn for 4 (9->5 life); plays Axel's Strategy (4 souls) standing Jormuntide Ignis again, its second attack cancelled by Lucky Pal; more Rooby buff/draw cycles; Foxparks attacks for 1 (5->4 life). Turn 12: xNIGHTn deploys Chillet and Astegon, debuffs Jormuntide Ignis -1000 power; Astegon's attack on Jormuntide Ignis nullified by Bushi interrupt (1 soul). Turn 13: xNIGHTn deploys Ragnahawk (6 souls), buffs all LavenousCAM's Red Pals +500/+1 strike (again, likely a recurring negative-for-self / opponent-buff mechanic tied to a specific card). Turn 14: Ragnahawk's attack interrupted by Blazehowl Noct; LavenousCAM's Jormuntide Ignis attacks for 5, cancelled by Lucky Pal; rests 3 souls to pay cost, stands it, assigns it. Turn 15: xNIGHTn plays Pal Sphere. Turn 16: xNIGHTn again buffs all LavenousCAM's Red Pals +500/+1 strike. Turn 17: LavenousCAM's Ragnahawk (buffed) attacks for 3 (4->1 life); Jormuntide Ignis (buffed) attacks for 5, life hits 1->0.

---------------------------------------------------------------------
A long 35-turn grindy game. Turn 1: AugMan spends souls for gear-cost reduction, deploys Pump-Action Shotgun (4 souls, deals 1200 to own Pals), discards Single-Shot Rifle; Chillet's attack interrupted by Reindrix. Turns 2-13: Repeated cycle -- Torrens23 plays Zoe's Strategy/Strike from the Darkness to recur and sacrifice Chillet/Relaxaurus, deploying and losing Relaxaurus twice (destroyed each time as Strike from the Darkness cost), Chillet's attack cancelled by Lucky Pal at one point, Relaxaurus's attack interrupted by Pyrin Noct. Torrens23's Relaxaurus attacks AugMan for 3 (10->7 life) on turn 7. Turn 14: Torrens23 deploys Elphidran Aqua, hits AugMan for 2 (4->2 life - AugMan had been worn down over many turns); deploys Chillet (5 souls), reveals/deploys Jormuntide Ignis, more gear-cost-reduction plays, deploys another Pump-Action Shotgun (self-damage 1200). Turn 15: AugMan's Jormuntide Ignis attack cancelled by Lucky Pal; AugMan discards cards to pay a cost and stands a card. Turns 16-28: Extended midgame -- Torrens23 plays Victor's Strategy (tutor), deploys Relaxaurus, Foxparks + Foxparks' Harness (buffed twice), rests souls; AugMan's Foxparks attacks Torrens23 for 2 (7->5 life); various Jormuntide Ignis attacks cancelled by Lucky Pal or interrupted by Reindrix; Torrens23 deploys Jormuntide (regular) twice via Aurora Guide plays, more gear-reduction Pump-Action Shotgun/Single-Shot Rifle deploys with self-damage; Torrens23 plays Zoe's Strategy again returning Chillet, discards Foxparks. Turn 29: Torrens23 plays Aurora Guide, deploys Chillet and Elphidran Aqua; Elphidran Aqua (900) blocked and destroyed by AugMan's Jormuntide Ignis (1700); Chillet (900) trades and mutually destroys with AugMan's blocking Chillet (900); Torrens23's Jormuntide attacks AugMan's Jormuntide Ignis. Turn 30: nullified by Reindrix interrupt (1 soul). Turn 31: Foxparks buffed +200 twice. Turn 32: AugMan's Foxparks attacks Torrens23 for 2 (5->3 life); Jormuntide Ignis attack interrupted by Reindrix, rests/stands souls; deploys another Foxparks, buffs Jormuntide Ignis; Foxparks (500 buffed) and Torrens23's Jormuntide (1600) mutually destroy each other (1900 dmg vs 1600 power, 1600 dmg vs 500 power). Turn 33: Torrens23 plays Victor's Strategy (tutor) then Zoe's Strategy. Turn 34: Torrens23 returns Relaxaurus from graveyard, discards Primitive Furnace. Turn 35: Torrens23 plays Pal Sphere -- and runs out of cards in deck, ending the game by deck-out.

PROSE SUMMARY (context, not a substitute for the structured log above)
---------------------------------------------------------------------
32-turn game. Porkchop built a Grappling Gun/Primitive Furnace/Single-Shot Rifle engine and repeatedly deployed and pinged down Reptyro copies while chip-attacking with Ragnahawk/Gobfin Ignis/Reptyro. GizmoHey's Blazehowl Noct/Foxparks/Reindrix/Pyrin Noct interrupted several of Porkchop's attacks (turns 5, 7, 9, 21, 27, 31). Combat trades: Katress destroyed GizmoHey's Foxparks (turn 3); GizmoHey's Shadowbeak/Tombat combo destroyed Porkchop's Reptyro in a double-block (turn 11); Ragnahawk destroyed GizmoHey's Leezpunk and Lyleen Noct (turns 14, 20); GizmoHey's Relaxaurus destroyed Porkchop's Flame Cauldron (turn 17) before being pinged down itself (turn 19); GizmoHey's own Shadowbeak butchered its Shadowbeak and Tombat for card advantage (turn 27). Porkchop closed with Ragnahawk/Gobfin Ignis/Reptyro chip damage (10->7->3->0).

PROSE SUMMARY (context, not a substitute for the structured log above)
---------------------------------------------------------------------
Long grindy game (44 turns). Jsn built a Grappling Gun / Stone Pit / Mounted Machine Gun / Primitive Furnace engine that repeatedly pinged down Carbon's big threats (Lyleen Noct destroyed twice via 500-dmg pings, Relaxaurus destroyed via pings, Astegon destroyed twice via pings, Elphidran Aqua destroyed via pings) while chip-attacking with Suzaku/Lamball/Flambelle/Jormuntide Ignis for repeated 1-4 damage hits (Carbon 10->9->8->7->6->5). Several of Jsn's own attacks were interrupted by Carbon's Reindrix/Cryolinx/Blazehowl Noct/Bushi/Foxparks. Carbon's Astegon and Zoe's Strategy/Foxparks combo destroyed some of Jsn's threats (Jormuntide Ignis, Chillet via butcher). Late game Jsn played Pengullet Rocket Launcher explode effect dealing 1100 to own board (self-wipe/reset play), then deployed Reptyro Cryst and attacked again with Suzaku for 3 before Carbon surrendered at 5 life.

PROSE SUMMARY (context, not a substitute for the structured log above)
---------------------------------------------------------------------
35-turn game. Zikale's Elphidran Aqua destroyed Trxvor's Lyleen Noct early via a double-KO trade (turn 3). Zikale built a heavy Pump-Action Shotgun/Single-Shot Rifle buff engine on Elphidran, hitting Trxvor for 2 (turn 23) then finishing Lyleen Noct in a mutual trade before Jormuntide Ignis chipped Trxvor down (9->7->3, turn 26). Trxvor's Chillet/Astegon/Pengullet then ground Zikale from 10 down to 0 (turns 27, 33, 35).

PROSE SUMMARY (context, not a substitute for the structured log above)
---------------------------------------------------------------------
Long 19-turn game dominated by Opponent's Primitive Furnace/Single-Shot Rifle ping engine (destroyed own overcosted threats intentionally?) and repeated "exploding Pengullet" plays (Pengullet + explode skill, dealing self-damage to own board: 1000, then 2200, then 2500 dealt to Opponent's own Pals across turns 9/13/15 - a deliberate board-wipe/reset tech). By turn 18 Opponent still had 9 life but a wide, low-power board (mostly freshly-deployed Pengullets); Trxvor's own Pengullet chipped Opponent for 1 (10 to 9) on turn 19, after which Opponent surrendered despite the life lead (9 vs Trxvor's 4) - likely a resignation on board state / hand exhaustion rather than life total.

Bandit's Relaxaurus attacks were nullified 4 separate times (turns 19, 20, 23, 26) by Jsn's interrupt chain, and eventually Bandit surrendered still at 3 life to Jsn's 10.

PROSE SUMMARY (context, not a substitute for the structured log above)
---------------------------------------------------------------------
27-turn game. Trxvor's early Pengullet chipped BDog down (10->8->6, turns 4/6) while BDog built up a wide Elphidran Aqua/Lyleen/Chillet board. Trxvor's Reindrix, Blazehowl Noct, and Cryolinx repeatedly nullified BDog's Elphidran Aqua and Lyleen swings via paid-Soul full-form interrupts (turns 10, 18, 20, 23) and one discard-cost Blazehowl Noct interrupt (turn 25) - no bare-form interrupts occurred this game. BDog's Chillet chipped Trxvor down turn 12 (6 to 4 own life via Lucky-cancelled Elphidran Aqua swing), then BDog's Digtoise/Mau Cryst/Elphidran Aqua/Pengullet combined for a decisive turn-26/27 finish: Digtoise's first hit (10 to 7), Mau Cryst and double Elphidran Aqua strikes (7->6->4->2), a Digtoise-vs-blocking-Pengullet trade with Lucky-cancelled breakthrough, and a final Pengullet hit for lethal (2 to 0).

PROSE SUMMARY (context, not a substitute for the structured log above)
---------------------------------------------------------------------
24-turn game, unusually one-sided in Lucky Pal cancels favoring Faraway (six of thegrumpygamerdad's attacks were cancelled across the game). thegrumpygamerdad's Univolt/Suzaku/Reptyro repeatedly attacked but were mostly Lucky-cancelled or bare/full-form interrupted (Reindrix turn 6, Foxparks turn 8, paid-Soul Cryolinx/Foxparks turn 13), while Faraway's Pengullet/Elphidran/Elphidran Aqua chipped thegrumpygamerdad down (10->8->6, turns 8/15) despite each Elphidran eventually being destroyed by thegrumpygamerdad's Primitive Furnace/Grappling Gun combo (turns 12, 16, 19). thegrumpygamerdad's Jormuntide Ignis attacks were twice nullified by paid-Soul Reindrix (turn 19) before Faraway's own Jormuntide destroyed it in combat turn 21, following a bare-form Cryolinx interrupt earlier that turn and an Axel's Strategy stand-refresh enabling a second Chillet swing (6->4). thegrumpygamerdad's own turn-23 Pump-Action Shotgun self-damage weakened his board further, and Faraway's Jormuntide closed it out turn 24 (2 to 0) after a final bare-form Reindrix interrupt on Chillet's attack.
